Memory is bounded by the per-eval log cap\n   enforced upstream by the orchestrator (T03.15), so handing the full\n   buffer down is safe.\n\n5. **Symmetric test matrix across the two primitives.** Both streams\n   get all 11 argument-coverage cases (contains × pass/fail, regex ×\n   pass/fail, not_contains × pass/fail, plus the ANSI-strip variants\n   on both, plus the envelope + declarative checks). The\nreturned callable has type `ExpectCallable = Callable[[EvalContext],\nExpectResult]` (DM-009) and carries `__name__ == \"duration\"` so the\nrunner's JSONL log records the originating primitive.\n\n## Behaviour matrix\n\n| `max_sec` | `min_sec` | Pass condition | Mode |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `None` | `None` | always pass | informational |\n| set | `None` | `observed <= max_sec` | upper-bound only |\n| `None` | set | `observed >= min_sec` | lower-bound only |\n| set | set | `min_sec <= observed <= max_sec` | windowed |\n\n`observed` is `ctx.duration_sec`, surfaced verbatim in\n`ExpectResult.details[\"observed_sec\"]` on every path (pass, fail,\ninformational) so the Reporter (T03.13) can chart benchmark numbers\nregardless of whether the assertion fired.\n\n## Informational-PASS semantics\n\nThe COMP-010.6 acceptance criterion says: *\"when only one bound is set,\nthe primitive records duration informationally even if the (missing)\nother bound would have failed.\"* The implementation honours this by\ntreating the absent bound as \"no claim\" rather than \"bound = 0\" or\n\"bound = ∞\":\n\n* `Expect.duration(max_sec=5)` against `duration_sec=0.0` → PASS, even\n  though a hypothetical `min_sec=1` would have failed.\n* `Expect.duration(min_sec=1)` against `duration_sec=10_000.0` → PASS,\n  even though a hypothetical `max_sec=10` would have failed.\n* `Expect.duration()` (no bounds) → PASS with message\n  `\"duration={observed:.3f}s (informational)\"`. The empty-args invocation\n  is the canonical \"I want this charted but I don't want to fail the\n  run\" entry point.\n\n`test_duration_max_only_does_not_fail_on_missing_min_bound` and\n`test_duration_min_only_does_not_fail_on_missing_max_bound` pin the\nsingle-bound informational behaviour. The no-bounds variants\n(`test_duration_no_bounds_passes_informationally`,\n`test_duration_no_bounds_records_zero_duration`) pin the zero-args path.\n\n## Boundary inclusivity\n\nBoth bounds are **inclusive**:\n\n* `observed == max_sec` → PASS (the failure check is strict `>`).\n* `observed == min_sec` → PASS (the failure check is strict `<`).\n\nThis matches manifest authors' intuition (set `max_sec=3` to mean\n\"three seconds is allowed, four is not\"). `test_duration_max_sec_passes_at_boundary`\nand `test_duration_min_sec_passes_at_boundary` pin the boundary\ncontract.\n\n## Evaluation order\n\nWhen both bounds are set and both are violated (e.g. `min_sec=10,\nmax_sec=3` with `observed=5`), `max_sec` is evaluated first. The\nfailure payload pins the over-budget bound regardless of which bound\nthe manifest author considered \"more wrong\" — this matches the most\ncommon operational concern (eval ran longer than budget) and gives\nthe report a single actionable bound to surface.\n`test_duration_both_bounds_max_evaluated_before_min` pins this.\n\n## Failure payload\n\n| Branch | `expected` | `actual` | Message |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| over budget | `{\"max_sec\": float}` | `float` (observed seconds) | `f\"duration {observed:.3f}s exceeds max_sec {max_sec}\"` |\n| under floor | `{\"min_sec\": float}` | `float` (observed seconds) | `f\"duration {observed:.3f}s below min_sec {min_sec}\"` |\n\n`ExpectFailure.traceback` is `None` for a predicate miss — a duration\nbreach is an ordinary assertion failure, not an exception, so the\nReporter does not render a stack trace.\n`test_duration_failure_traceback_field_is_none_for_predicate_miss`\npins this contract.\n\n## Test matrix (`tests/cli/eval/test_expect_duration.py`)\n\n| # | Test | Mode | Direction |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | `test_duration_no_bounds_passes_informationally` | no bounds | PASS |\n| 2 | `test_duration_no_bounds_records_zero_duration` | no bounds, 0s edge | PASS |\n| 3 | `test_duration_max_sec_passes_when_under_budget` | `max_sec` only | PASS |\n| 4 | `test_duration_max_sec_passes_at_boundary` | `max_sec` boundary | PASS |\n| 5 | `test_duration_max_sec_fails_when_over_budget` | `max_sec` only | FAIL |\n| 6 | `test_duration_min_sec_passes_when_above_floor` | `min_sec` only | PASS |\n| 7 | `test_duration_min_sec_passes_at_boundary` | `min_sec` boundary | PASS |\n| 8 | `test_duration_min_sec_fails_when_below_floor` | `min_sec` only | FAIL |\n| 9 | `test_duration_both_bounds_pass_when_within_window` | both bounds | PASS |\n| 10 | `test_duration_both_bounds_fail_via_max_sec` | both bounds | FAIL (max) |\n| 11 | `test_duration_both_bounds_fail_via_min_sec` | both bounds | FAIL (min) |\n| 12 | `test_duration_both_bounds_max_evaluated_before_min` | evaluation order | FAIL (max) |\n| 13 | `test_duration_max_only_does_not_fail_on_missing_min_bound` | informational on missing | PASS |\n| 14 | `test_duration_min_only_does_not_fail_on_missing_max_bound` | informational on missing | PASS |\n| 15 | `test_from_mapping_threads_duration_max_sec` | declarative form | PASS |\n| 16 | `test_from_mapping_threads_duration_both_bounds` | declarative form | PASS |\n| 17 | `test_from_mapping_threads_duration_no_bounds` | declarative form | PASS |\n| 18 | `test_duration_result_carries_primitive_name_and_timing` | DM-009 envelope | PASS |\n| 19 | `test_duration_failure_traceback_field_is_none_for_predicate_miss` | failure payload | FAIL |\n\nAll 19 cases pass under\n`uv run pytest tests/cli/eval/test_expect_duration.py -v` in 0.19 s on\n2026-05-21.\n\n## Declarative form\n\n`Expect.from_mapping({\"duration\": {...}})` resolves to the same\ncallable as the programmatic form. Three declarative shapes are\nsupported:\n\n```yaml\nexpects:\n  - duration: {}                          # informational PASS\n  - duration: {max_sec: 3.0}              # upper bound only\n  - duration: {min_sec: 1.0, max_sec: 3.0}  # windowed\n```\n\nThe empty mapping `{}` is the manifest sugar for the zero-args\ninformational form. `test_from_mapping_threads_duration_no_bounds`,\n`test_from_mapping_threads_duration_max_sec`, and\n`test_from_mapping_threads_duration_both_bounds` pin all three shapes.\n\n## Downstream consumers\n\n* Benchmarking manifests — `duration: {}` records observed timings on\n  every eval without forcing an assertion, so the Reporter can produce\n  a \"duration trend\" panel from the artifact tree.\n* T05.x (eval suite) — evals that have a known operational budget pin\n  `duration: {max_sec: N}` so a regression that doubles eval runtime\n  surfaces as FAIL even if the functional Expects still pass.\n\n## Linked roadmap entries\n\n* R-070 — COMP-010.6 / D-0070\n* Depends on: T04.01 (Expect package skeleton, D-0064)\n* Used by: T05.x manifests; benchmarking surface (post-M5)\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01A872V35ELghkFqDaNbr8iJ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01NpwnBP6HJqary3bJGQWKwZ","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/config/workspace/IronClaude/.dev/releases/current/cliEval/artifacts/D-0070/notes.md","content":"# D-0070 — Implementation notes\n\n## Why this task was test-only\n\nT04.01 (D-0064) shipped the full `Expect.duration` body alongside the\npackage skeleton. The primitive already supported:\n\n* Two named arguments: `max_sec` and `min_sec` (each `Optional[float]`,\n  default `None`).\n* The four-mode behaviour matrix (no bounds / max only / min only /\n  both bounds).\n* Failure payloads with `expected` / `actual` (DM-005), inclusive\n  boundary semantics, and `max_sec`-first evaluation order.\n* `observed_sec` surfaced in `ExpectResult.details` on every path.\n\nT04.08's deliverable is the **per-primitive acceptance harness** that\ntouches every mode in the behaviour matrix, the informational-PASS\nsemantics for the missing-bound case, and the DM-009 envelope. No body\nchanges were required to `src/superclaude/cli/eval/expect.py`.\n\n## Decisions\n\n1. **Inclusive boundaries (`<`/`>` not `<=`/`>=`).** `observed ==\n   max_sec` is PASS rather than FAIL. Manifest authors who write\n   `max_sec=3` mean \"three seconds is allowed,\" and the inclusive\n   reading matches the colloquial intent. The two boundary tests\n   (`test_duration_max_sec_passes_at_boundary`,\n   `test_duration_min_sec_passes_at_boundary`) pin both ends so any\n   future change to strict comparisons fails the suite.\n\n2. **Single-bound missing-bound is informational, not zero/∞.** When\n   only `max_sec` is set, the missing `min_sec` is treated as \"no\n   claim\" rather than \"min_sec=0\" or \"min_sec=-∞\". A 0-second eval\n   against `max_sec=5` PASSes — there is no implicit floor. Symmetric\n   on the `min_sec` side. This is the COMP-010.6 informational-PASS\n   contract and the two dedicated tests\n   (`test_duration_max_only_does_not_fail_on_missing_min_bound`,\n   `test_duration_min_only_does_not_fail_on_missing_max_bound`) pin it\n   so a future refactor that \"tightens\" the missing bound to a\n   zero-floor would visibly fail.\n\n3. **`max_sec` evaluated before `min_sec` when both are violated.**\n   The over-budget case is more operationally actionable (the eval ran\n   longer than the harness's wall-clock budget), so when both bounds\n   are violated the failure payload pins `max_sec`. This is exercised\n   even on the degenerate `min_sec > max_sec` window via\n   `test_duration_both_bounds_max_evaluated_before_min` so the\n   evaluation order survives any future bound-validation refactor.\n\n4. **`observed_sec` is in `details` on every path.** Even when the\n   assertion fails or runs informationally, the observed duration is\n   handed to the Reporter unconditionally so dashboards / JUnit\n   payloads can chart timings without re-parsing the artifact tree.\n   `test_duration_max_sec_fails_when_over_budget` explicitly asserts\n   `result.details[\"observed_sec\"] == 5.0` on the FAIL path so this\n   contract cannot regress silently.\n\n5. **No-args is the canonical informational form.** `Expect.duration()`\n   with no bounds returns `passed=True` with message\n   `\"duration={observed:.3f}s (informational)\"`. This is the explicit\n   \"record but don't assert\" entry point — manifests that just want\n   timing data on the report attach `duration: {}` to every eval\n   without needing a sentinel `max_sec=float(\"inf\")` workaround.\n\n6. **`ExpectFailure.traceback` is `None` for predicate misses.** An\n   over-budget or under-floor duration is an ordinary assertion\n   failure, not an exception. The Reporter (T03.13) uses the presence\n   of `traceback` to decide whether to render a stack-trace section,\n   and a duration breach should not produce one.\n   `test_duration_failure_traceback_field_is_none_for_predicate_miss`\n   pins this contract.\n\n## What was NOT changed in src/\n\n* `Expect.duration` body in `src/superclaude/cli/eval/expect.py` was\n  already complete after T04.01. The T04.08 acceptance suite is\n  purely additive.\n* `_named_callable`, `_timed_result`, and `_make_failure` helpers were\n  untouched.\n\n## Followups deferred\n\n* Manifest-loader smoke test pairing `Expect.from_mapping` with a\n  fully assembled `expects:` block that includes a `duration` entry\n  (planned alongside the manifest loader landing in T05.x).\n* Reporter rendering of the `observed_sec` field on the informational\n  path (planned alongside the TEST-007 reporter contract suite,\n  T04.17).\n* Duration aggregation across a suite (mean / p95) for the\n  benchmarking dashboard (post-M5).\n\n## Linked artifacts\n\n* Spec: `D-0070/spec.md`\n* Evidence: `evidence/T04.08/pytest-output.txt`\n* Source: `src/superclaude/cli/eval/expect.py` (`Expect.duration`)\n* Test module: `tests/cli/eval/test_expect_duration.py`\n* Roadmap: R-070, depends on T04.01 (D-0064)\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3215,"cache_read_input_tokens":134864,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3215,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":46,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available","speed":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"164ecaa2-6165-4cd9-9795-61785328aaf0","uuid":"f59cb4a4-3549-46ce-879d-cd0f1c4e9ffb"}
